# Spaceman
Aliases: Astronaut
Categories: AHN, Non-player characters, Confirmations needed
This page contains content that has recently been updated on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], but that also exists on the main Hypixel network.
[Spaceman]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Watches the player's Garden Visitor ([[Garden Visitors]]) progress.
Location: [[The Observatory]]
Coordinates: 260.5 182 -565.5
Description: Appears as a Garden Visitor ([[Garden Visitors]]).
Location: [[The Garden]]
Spaceman is an [[NPC]] that only appears in [[The Garden]]. They have a 0.0439% (1 in 2277.67) chance to appear after the player has reached [[The Garden|Garden XV (15)]] and has served 75 unique [[Garden Visitors]].
Every 3,000th visitor on [[The Garden]] is guaranteed to be Spaceman, assuming the player otherwise meets the requirements.
## Mechanics
Spaceman asks for a random crop, with the amount of items they want being as close to 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]] [[NPC]] sell price as possible in the highest tier compacted form of the crop.
Accepting guarantees +100,000 [[Farming|Farming XP]], 75 Garden XP, 100 [[Copper]] and a [[Space Helmet]] as a reward. If Spaceman was [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set|charmed]] during their previous visit, their next visit also rewards an [[Minion Skins|Astronaut Minion Skin]].
|Items Requested|Raw Materials|Relative Crop Amount|
|-|-|-|
|6,510 (101 stacks plus 46) [[Enchanted Hay Bale]]|166,656,000 (2,604,000 stacks) [[Wheat]]|166,656,000|
|13,021 (203 stacks plus 29) [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|333,337,600 (5,208,400 stacks) [[Carrot]]|111,112,533|
|13,021 [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|333,337,600 [[Potato]]|111,112,533|
|3,906 (61 stacks plus 2) [[Polished Pumpkin]]|99,993,600 (1,562,400 stacks) [[Pumpkin]]|99,993,600|
|9,766 (152 stacks plus 38) [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|250,009,600 (3,906,400 stacks) [[Sugar Cane]]|125,004,800|
|19,531 (305 stacks plus 11) [[Enchanted Melon]]|499,993,600 (7,812,400 stacks) [[Melon Slice]]|99,998,720|
|9,766 [[Enchanted Cactus]]|250,009,600 [[Cactus]]|125,004,800|
|13,021 [[Enchanted Cookie]]|333,337,600 [[Cocoa Beans]]|111,112,533|
|3,906 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]]|99,993,600 Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|99,993,600|
|3,906 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]]|99,993,600 Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|99,993,600|
|9,766 [[Mutant Nether Wart]]|250,009,600 [[Nether Wart]]|100,003,840|
|9,766 [[Compacted Moonflower]]|250,009,600 [[Moonflower]]|125,004,800|
|9,766 [[Compacted Sunflower]]|250,009,600 [[Sunflower]]|125,004,800|
|9,766 [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|250,009,600 [[Wild Rose]]|125,004,800|
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction with garden locked [AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.)|You must complete the Garden tutorial before using this! [NPC] Spaceman: Have you heard of the Garden? [NPC] Spaceman: Well I'm sure you'll have your own soon enough. [NPC] Spaceman: When you do, come talk to me.|
|First interaction with garden unlocked [AHN]|[NPC] Spaceman: So you've got your own Garden, but are you shotting for the Stars? [NPC] Spaceman: Serve 3,000 Visitors in your Garden and then come back.|
|Trying to give [[Bachelor's Rose]] while not garden 15 [AHN]|You do not have enough Garden Experience to give a rose to this person|
|After being given a [[Bachelor's Rose]] from a player who has served fewer than 3,000 [[Garden Visitors]] [AHN]|You do not meet the requirements to give a rose to this person.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|After being given a [[Bachelor's Rose]] from a player who served 3,000 [[Garden Visitors]] or more [AHN]|[NPC] Spaceman: From up here I'm able to see all the Gardens in SkyBlock. [NPC] Spaceman: All the crops...all the Visitors... [NPC] Spaceman: Your Garden seems particularly of note, however. [NPC] Spaceman: You've accepted offers from well over 3,000 Visitors... [NPC] Spaceman: If you were to invite me to visit, I would not say no.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Spaceman: Man, coming back here after being in space for so long feels really good. [NPC] Spaceman: Something I have been craving for a while is [crop], they unfortunately do not grow in space.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Spaceman: You are amazing, thank you so much.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Spaceman: I am quite disappointed.|
## Trivia
- The first player to complete a trade with the Spaceman was 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] TGWaffles.
- The Spaceman accepts any quantity of the required items until the player has given the full amount.
  - The player can still refuse their offer after giving some items, however the previously given items will not be refunded.
- The Spaceman was the first [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]].

# Awakened Summoning Eye
Categories: AHN Exclusive, Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Confirmations needed
This page contains content that was exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], and has been removed since its introduction. It will likely never make it to the main Hypixel network.
[Awakened Summoning Eye]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 5x [[Summoning Eye]] 4x [[Null Ovoid]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AWAKENED_SUMMONING_EYE
Tooltip: Use this at the Ender Altar in the Dragon's Nest to summon special Ender Dragons!; LEGENDARY
Awakened Summoning Eye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be used to summon [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragons]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x [[Null Ovoid]] 5x [[Summoning Eye]]|Recipe: [[Summoning Eye]] ×5 + [[Null Ovoid]] ×4 → Awakened Summoning Eye|
### Special Zealots
[[Zealot|Special Zealots]] have a 4% chance to drop an Awakened Summoning Eye instead of a regular [[Summoning Eye]] when killed.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Usage
Awakened Summoning Eyes are used for summoning one of eight types of [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]]. Unlike a regular [[Summoning Eye]], multiple dragons spawn at the same time.
When a player right-clicks an empty portal frame in [[Dragon's Nest]] with an Awakened Summoning Eye, it will be placed and turned into a [[Sleeping Eye]], allowing for the player to retrieve the eye if needed. If the [[Sleeping Eye]] remains in its form after 8 eyes have been placed (one in each portal frame), it will turn into a [[Remnant of the Eye]].
Rarely, when a dragon is spawned, it triggers GOD MODE, during which 2 [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragons]] will always[Confirm] spawn.
Another possible rare occurrence is DRAGON RUSH, during which multiple waves of Dragons will always spawn. Always between 3-5 [Confirm]. Next wave of Dragons will spawn after the previous one was defeated, and players are rewarded after all the waves are done.
When an Awakened Summoning Eye is placed in the altar, regular [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] cannot be placed, and vice versa.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 28, 2024|AHN 0.19.12|Added Awakened Summoning Eye.|
|Feb 29, 2024|Crafting recipe updated from 8 [[Summoning Eye]] and 2 [[Null Ovoid]] to 5 [[Summoning Eye]] and 4 [[Null Ovoid]].|
|May 3, 2024|Special Zealots ([[Special Zealot]]) now have a 4% chance to drop an Awakened Summoning Eye.|
|May 3, 2024|Voidgloom Seraphs ([[Voidgloom Seraph]]) now have a small chance to drop an Awakened Summoning Eye.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Announced that the Primal Dragons update is shelved for the time being, due to large scale performance issues.|
|Jun 8, 2026|Q&A Summary mentioned that there is no plans about the update, as it would require rewriting parts of the game due to aforementioned technical issues. Also mentioned the possibility of making more [[The End]] content in the future, but with no plans being done or in the process of being made.|
Linked from: [[Eye (disambiguation)]], [[Primal (disambiguation)]], [[Special Zealot]], [[Voidgloom Seraph]]

# Barter Bank Show
Categories: AHN Exclusive, Locations
This page contains content that is exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] and will never be released on the main Hypixel network.
[Barter Bank Show]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
← Previous Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Cashier]] [[Game Agent]]
[Metadata]
ID: BARTER_BANK
The Barter Bank Show was a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It was removed upon the closing of the Alpha network once testing for the Rift had been completed.
## Features
The Barter Bank Show featured a few attractions.
### Cashier
The [[Cashier]] was an [[NPC]] located in the Barter Bank Show. The player could talk to them to trade 10,000 [[Motes]] for 20M NotCoins, a currency exclusive to the Barter Bank Show.
### Barter Bank Show
Despite never being released, the Barter Bank Show contained two [[NPC|NPCs]] which hinted at a game show. The game could have been started by talking to the [[Game Agent]], and 10 chests would appear, containing unique amounts of NotCoins. The goal would have been for the player to sell their chest for as much as possible to The Merchant, an unknown character.
The steps were as follows:
1. Pick YOUR chest. 2. Open 3 chests. 3. Receive OFFER from The Merchant. 3.5. Accept or Decline the offer. 4. Open more chests, get more offers... 5. If all offers declined, open YOUR chest.
## Trivia
Despite never being released, the Barter Bank Show structure is still in the rift, near the [[Mirrorverse]] underground.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Barter Bank Show.|
Linked from: [[Cashier]], [[Empty Bank]], [[Game Agent]], [[Locations]]

The Giant's Eye Sword served as an upgraded form of the [[Giant's Sword]]. The Eye Beam ability functioned identically to the [[Precursor Eye]], activating on right-click instead of sneak. All damage dealt to mobs by the laser were multiplied by 1.5x when the [[Precursor Eye]] was worn.

# God Potion Effect
Categories: AHN Exclusive, More info needed, Confirmations needed
God Potion Effect is a series of effects granted mainly by consuming the [[God Potion]].
## Obtaining
### God Potion
Consuming a [[God Potion]] grants all of its effects for a default period of 12 hours, which can be increased up to 24 hours via [[Alchemy]] skill levels and by an additional 20% by having a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Parrot Pet]]. The maximum duration of the effects from a single God Potion is 28 hours and 48 minutes. Consuming multiple God Potions increases the duration of the effects, but this cannot exceed 192 hours (8 days). Attempting to consume a God Potion which would increase the duration beyond this limit will be prevented.
### Centauri
This page's section contains content that is exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], and will never be released on the main Hypixel Network.
Via [[Centauri]]'s Toy Box, players can get 292,471,155 years, 208 days, 9 hours and 15 minutes of these same effects.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Is that still the duration?; When this info is added, remove this.)
## List of Effects
|Potion Effect|Description in-game|Granted Bonus|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|

|Stun IV ([[Stun Potion]])|When applied to yourself, your hits have a 40% chance to stun enemies for 1s. When splashed, enemies are stunned for 1.75s.|No effect[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]||

In total, the granted stats are: 100 [[Health]], 375 [[Absorption]], 66 [[Defense]], 20 [[True Defense]], 98.75 [[Strength]], 25 [[Crit Chance]], 80 [[Crit Damage]], 2 [[Ferocity]], 100 [[Intelligence]], 31.5 [[Health Regen]], 200 [[Mining Speed]] (on certain islands), 228 [[Speed]], 88 [[Magic Find]], 20 [[Pet Luck]], 30 [[Combat Wisdom]], 20 [[Farming Wisdom]], 20 [[Fishing Wisdom]], 20 [[Mining Wisdom]], 20 [[Foraging Wisdom]], 20 [[Enchanting Wisdom]] and 20 [[Alchemy Wisdom]]. Additionally it grants the following bonuses: recovering 8 [[Mana]] and 45 [[Health]] per second, having 56%[Confirm] chance to dodge, increasing bow damage by 80%, 40% more Experience Orbs, increasing jump height by 4.92 blocks and immunity to drowning outside of [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Notes
- The duration of the God Potion Effect doesn't drop while the player is on any player's [[Private Island]], the effect will however remain. The duration is also paused inside the [[Catacombs]], but so will be the effect.
- The God Potion Effect does not dissipate when dying.
- Using any regular potion (any potion that is not a God Potion) while under the effects of a God Potion will not increase the effect's timer, even if the God Potion has less time on it than the potion being used would give. This renders all potions included in a God Potion useless while it is active.
- Players can toggle off the effects granted by God Potion by navigating SkyBlock Menu → Booster Cookie → Toggle Potion Effects. This requires an active [[Booster Cookie]].
- If a player drinks a [[Milk Bucket]] or a [[Magical Milk Bucket]], all potion effects will be removed.
- The duration is not affected by any [[Potion Affinity Talisman|Potion Affinity Accessories]], but is still affected by the [[Parrot Pet|Parrot Pet's]] fourth ability, "Parrot Feather Infusion".
- The [[Absorption]] from [[Absorption Potion|Absorption]] and [[Adrenaline Potion|Adrenaline]] doesn't stack together, resulting in only the former (and the stronger of the two) taking effect.
## Trivia
- There used to be a bug where when a player was warped directly from one [[Dungeons]] server to another, there was a small chance that they will lose all buffs from a God Potion, despite the Tab list and SkyBlock Menu still showing that it is active. This has been patched since then.
- During the release of the [[Chili Pepper]] update, there was a bug causing God Potions to grant the [[Entropy Suppressor|Coldfused Reforge]] as a potion effect.
  - It lasted for the full duration of the God Potion. This means that even after it was patched, players could still keep the effect until the potion ran out.
- [[Speed Potion|Speed VIII]] is boosted by [[Decent Coffee]], despite [[Black Coffee]] having a stronger effect.
- [[Bitter Iced Tea]] and [[Red Thornleaf Tea]] are not used as components of [[God Potion]], despite being applicable to some of its effects.
- If a [[Brews|Brew]] is applied to multiple drunk potions, all the drunk potions will have their effects increased with a Brew. Despite this, God Potion never uses the same Brew multiple times.
  - If [[Black Coffee]] was used to brew [[Adrenaline Potion|Adrenaline]], [[Agility Potion|Agility]], [[Rabbit Potion|Rabbit]] and [[Speed Potion|Speed]] potion, the Brew would grant a total of 48 [[Speed]], but since only 1 [[Decent Coffee]] is used, that Speed is lost.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 31, 2024|0.19.11 RC|Drinking multiple God Potions will now extend the duration rather than refreshing it.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added God Potion.|
|Sep 9, 2020|God Potion now includes brews|
|Jan 14, 2021|Added [[Jerry Candy]]'s effects to God Potion.|
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Spelunker IV ([[Spelunker Potion]]) to God Potion.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Newly bought God Potions now last only 12 hours, but gain up to 12 bonus hours based on [[Alchemy]] level.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Existing God Potions are now turned into legacy God Potions, which last 24 hours as before.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Effects of God Potion are now extended by the [[Parrot Pet]].|
|Mar 12, 2021|God Potions can now be improved with [[Mixins]] in a [[Brewing Stand]].|
|Mar 18, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers.|
|Mar 29, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers again.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Drinking multiple God Potions will now extend the duration rather than refreshing it.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|God Potions can be consumed to last up to 192 hours (8 days).|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|God Potions no longer count down while on a [[Private Island]].|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Fixed God Potions not having rarity tag.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added Healing IX ([[Healing Potion]]).|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Nerfed Regeneration ([[Regeneration Potion]]) to half of its previous effect.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Removed [[Health]] recovery from [[Viking's Tear]].|

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Mino]]
Buy: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
Tooltip: Health: -1,337; Intelligence: +1,000; Hello and welcome to what is hopefully my final attempt at completing Hypixel SkyBlock without taking any damage. I have a max HP of 1 so any damage from any source will immediately kill me. Mobs also only have 1 HP to make the game more interesting. -OHKO!; LEGENDARY NECKLACE
One Hit Necklace was a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]].
## Obtaining
A One Hit Necklace was purchasable from [[Mino]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] while he was present in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
## Usage
Necklace:
One Hit Necklace [[Health]]: -1337 [[Intelligence]]: +1000
[Effects]
[Hello and welcome to what is hopefully my final attempt at beating Hypixel SkyBlock without taking any damage. I have a max HP of 1 so any damage from any source will immediately kill me. Mobs also only have 1 HP to make the game more interesting. -OHKO!]
## Trivia
- The One Hit Necklace's description is a reference to the Twitch streamer DarkViperAU and his one hit knockout (OHKO) series in Grand Theft Auto V where he played through the entire game without taking any damage. He began each episode with the following:
  - "Hello and welcome to what is hopefully my final attempt at completing GTA 5 without taking any damage. I have a max HP of 1, so any damage from any source will immediately kill me. I also want this to be a no-hit run, so Trevor's special ability is disabled because while it prevents you from taking damage, it does not prevent you from being hit by things. I have successfully completed every single mission without taking damage. I still have yet to do it all in one go. My current personal best for an entire run is one bit of damage and therefore one death. Obviously very close to a perfect run, so I'm hoping this time I get an actual perfect run and avoid all damage entirely."
- The One Hit Necklace's texture was reused for [[Hemoglass]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 14, 2021|Added One Hit Necklace.|

Categories: AHN Exclusive, Historical content, Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
This page contains content that is exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] and will never be released on the main Hypixel network.
Overgrown Dye was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Overgrown Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_OVERGROWN
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #7BAF34!; LEGENDARY DYE
Overgrown Dye was a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Overgrown Dye had a 0.01% (1 in 10,000) chance to be obtained from serving [[Vincent]] on [[The Garden]].
## Usage
Overgrown Dye could be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #7BAF34. It cost 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It could also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Usage
- Overgrown Dye was the only dye to not have the color on its lid correspond to its rarity.
## Gallery
- Overgrown Dyed Leather Armor
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Overgrown Dye.|
|Dec 10, 2025|Removed Overgrown Dye.|
Linked from: [[Dyes]], [[Vincent]]

Primal Dragon Armor was a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. [[Combat|Combat XXVI (26)]] was required to use it and, if the item was dungeonised, [[Catacombs|Catacombs XVI (16)]] to use it while in [[Dungeons]].
## Obtaining
Primal Dragon Armor pieces dropped from a [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragon]]. A randomly selected piece had a 30% (1 in 3.33) chance to drop if the player met the [[Ender Dragon|Dragon Weight]] requirement.
When Primal Dragon Armor was obtained as loot from a [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragon]], it was always [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]].

|Primal Dragon Leggings ([[Primal Dragon Armor]])|None|70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) Primal Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×7 → [[Primal Dragon Armor]]|
|Primal Dragon Boots ([[Primal Dragon Armor]])|None|40x Primal Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×4 → [[Primal Dragon Armor]]|

## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 28, 2024|AHN 0.19.12|Added Primal Dragon Armor.|
|Feb 29, 2024|Increased [[Combat]] level requirement from 22 to 26.|
|Feb 29, 2024|Nerfed each Primal Dragon Armor piece by -10 [[Defense]].|
|May 3, 2024|Primal Blood has been nerfed and now gives 4% armor stats per milestone instead of 20%.|
|May 3, 2024|Kill milestones are now at 50, 125, 250, 500, 1,000, and 2,000 Dragon Kills (rather than number of digits).|
|May 3, 2024|Each piece of Primal Dragon Armor has been given +4 [[Pet Luck]] at base (+16 total).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Announced that the Primal Dragons update is shelved for the time being, due to large scale performance issues.|
|Jun 8, 2026|Q&A Summary mentioned that there is no plans about the update, as it would require rewriting parts of the game due to aforementioned technical issues. Also mentioned the possibility of making more [[The End]] content in the future, but with no plans being done or in the process of being made.|
Linked from: [[Boots]], [[Chestplates]], [[Dragon (disambiguation)]], [[Gemstone Slot]], [[Gemstone Slot/DPL]], [[Helmets]], [[Leggings]], [[Primal (disambiguation)]]

Primal Dragon Egg is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
The Primal Dragon Egg has a 0.25% chance to drop from defeating a [[Ender Dragon|Primal Dragon]].
## Usage
The Primal Dragon Egg can be used to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ender Dragon Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] at [[Kat]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. The process will take 1 hour.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 28, 2024|AHN 0.19.12|Added Primal Dragon Egg.|

Also mentioned the possibility of making more [[The End]] content in the future, but with no plans being done or in the process of being made.|

# Rogier
Categories: AHN Exclusive, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
This page contains content that was exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], and has been removed since its introduction. It will likely never make it to the main Hypixel network.
[Rogier]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[The End]]
Coordinates: -587 103 -273
Rogier was an [[NPC]] in [[The End]] that would move to the [[Village]] after speaking to them. There, Rogier will allow the player to either extract, reset, or transfuse items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Rogier: Ah, nice to meet you. [NPC] Rogier: Rogier’s the name, and I have some intriguing news to share with you. [NPC] Rogier: I'm a researcher of sorts, specializing in the art of Transfusion. [NPC] Rogier: I'm currently seeking a particular artifact that is rumored to possess great power when paired with certain weapons. [NPC] Rogier: As you might expect, the journey has been fraught with danger. [NPC] Rogier: Perhaps adventurer, we can join forces in our quests, for safety in numbers, if nothing else. [NPC] Rogier: So, what do you say? Select an option: [I Accept] [I Decline]|
|Selecting [I Decline]|[NPC] Rogier: Ah, you are not ready I presume. I respect your decision. I'll continue my search for the artifact on my own. [NPC] Rogier: But do keep in mind that this place is fraught with danger, and it's always better to have a partner by your side. [NPC] Rogier: If you change your mind, I'll be around. Best of luck on your own quest.|
|Selecting [I Accept]|[NPC] Rogier: Excellent, adventurer! [NPC] Rogier: Together we'll be a force to be reckoned with. I'll share with you all that I've learned about the artifact and its potential. [NPC] Rogier: With your skills and my expertise, I'm confident that we'll find it in no time. [NPC] Rogier: Meet me in the Hub at once! We will then discuss our plans forward. I will seek shelter in the home of an old friend of mine.|
|In Hub After Selecting [I Accept]|[NPC] Rogier: I've had a recent conversation with an old acquaintance of mine, the Wizard. [NPC] Rogier: Apparently, there's a possibility of reconstructing the artifact we need by extracting the essence of certain weapons. [NPC] Rogier: And here's the exciting part - if we go through with this plan, I'll be able to transfuse any ability of the weapons you extract onto another weapon. [NPC] Rogier: This means you'll have a more versatile and adaptable build for your adventures. [NPC] Rogier: So, what do you say, adventurer? Select an option: [Let's do it] [It's too risky]|
|Selecting [Let's do it]|[NPC] Rogier: Wonderful, let’s shall try to extract the ability of a fairly basic weapon at first so I can enhance my craft, perhaps a Rogue Sword could do. [NPC] Rogier: Return to me at once when you acquire one, and be swift.|
|Selecting [It's too risky]|[NPC] Rogier: I understand your concern. [NPC] Rogier: It's never easy to take on a risky endeavor, especially one with an uncertain outcome. [NPC] Rogier: However, I believe that the potential reward far outweighs the risk. [NPC] Rogier: And with your skills and my expertise, we stand a good chance of success. [NPC] Rogier: But I also respect your decision [NPC] Rogier: If you ever change your mind, know that I'll be ready and willing to embark on this journey with you.|
|After Bringing A [[Rogue Sword]]|[NPC] Rogier: Great! I see you brought me the basic weapon. [NPC] Rogier: Let me try to extract its essence. [NPC] Rogier: Hand me the weapon.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Interacting After Selecting [I Decline]|[NPC] Rogier: Ah, welcome back, adventurer. I knew you'd see the wisdom in partnering up. [NPC] Rogier: Will you join me in my adventures? Select an option: [I Accept] [I Decline]|
|Interacting After Selecting [It's too risky]|[NPC] Rogier: So, what do you say, adventurer? [NPC] Rogier: Shall we take this risk and attempt to extract the essence of these weapons? [NPC] Rogier: The reward could be tremendous, and I'm confident that with your skills and my expertise, we'll make it happen. Select an option: [Let's do it] [It's too risky]|
## Trivia
- Rogier is most likely a reference to the character of the same name from Elden Ring.
  - Rogier's first line of dialogue is the same.
  - Additionally, they both share similar clothing and goals of searching for items in a dangerous location.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 10, 2023|Added Rogier.|

The Undying Fossil Shard was a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that granted the [[Attributes|Glacial Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Undying Fossil Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Glacial Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Glacial Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] on Glacial ([[Glacial (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Undying Fossil Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
## Lore
[More Info Needed]

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Undying Fossil Shard.|
|Jul 29, 2026|Removed Undying Fossil Shard.|

# April Fools/2020
Aliases: April Fools (2020), The April Fools Joke of 2020
Categories: April Fools
The Jerry skin
On April 1st, 2020, Hypixel admins temporarily changed everyone's skin to a Villager. The names of Hypixel ranks also temporarily changed.
## Ranks
|Original Rank|Joke Rank|
|-|-|
|2[VIP] ([[Ranks]])|22[JER] ([[Ranks]])|
|3[VIP+] ([[Ranks]])|23[JER] ([[Ranks]])|
|4[MVP] ([[Ranks]])|24[JRY] ([[Ranks]])|
|5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]])|25[JRY+] ([[Ranks]])|
|6[MVP++] ([[Ranks]])|26[JRY++] ([[Ranks]])|
|7[YOUTUBE] ([[Ranks]])|27[YERRY] ([[Ranks]])|
|8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]])|28[JERRY+++] ([[Ranks]])|
|52[HELPER] ([[Ranks]])|29[JERRY] ([[Ranks]])|
|53[MOD] ([[Ranks]])|30[JERRY] ([[Ranks]])|
|12[ADMIN] ([[Ranks]])|31[SR JERRY] ([[Ranks]])|
## Trivia
- This joke pays homage to the iconic [[Jerry]] [[NPC]] from SkyBlock. All ranks were reverted during April 1st 2020.
- [[Jerry]] can finally move freely around your [[Private Island]] on SkyBlock.
- The Owner rank was not renamed.
Linked from: [[April Fools]]

# April Fools/2022
Categories: April Fools
The Hub Map
Jerry Fans
Jerry Fans
On April 1st, 2022, Many changes/pranks happened around SkyBlock.
## Changes
- All player-skin NPCs were changed to a jerry skin.
- [[Pets]] without external [[Pet Skins]] changed to a jerry skin.
- [[Jerry]] can move freely around your [[Private Island]] on SkyBlock.
- GUIs changed all their usual black stained glass panes to a single random color.
- Cookie Clicker v0.01 was added on the [[Booster Cookie]] Menu.
- Added Jerry fans NPCs (see below).
- All [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|soundtracks]] are replaced by Jerry noises.
- Five NPCs named [[Jerry Fan]] situate themselves in the [[Hub Island|Hub]]. They surround a big picture of [[Jerry]] displaying on where the map is usually at. They hold a staring angle towards the center of the picture.
Linked from: [[April Fools]]

# April Fools/2023
Categories: April Fools
On April 1st, 2023, many temporary changes and jokes happened in SkyBlock.
## Changes
Minions were 2 blocks tall.
Scoreboard was showing "SKIBLOCK".
- All player-skin NPCs were changed to a Jerry skin.
- All [[Pets]] floating heads changed to a Jerry skin. Their appearance as an item (in the Pet Menu) was unchanged.
- [[Jerry]] could move freely around your [[Private Island]] on SkyBlock. Jerry could also jump up to around 4 blocks high.
- GUIs changed all their usual black stained glass panes to a single random color.
- [[Cookie Clicker]] v0.03 was released.
- All [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|soundtracks]] were replaced by Jerry noises.
- Certain, but not all sound effects were replaced with villager noises.
- SkyBlock was renamed to SkiBlock on the scoreboard.
- Minions were two blocks tall and had new dialogue.
- Five NPCs named [[Jerry Fan]] situated themselves in the [[Hub Island|Hub]]. They surrounded a big picture of [[Jerry]] displaying on where the map is usually at. They held a staring angle towards the center of the picture.
### SkyBlock Levels Chat Prefix
Colors for [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Levels Chat Prefix]] were reversed:
Jerry Fans surrounding the changed map.
|Level|Color during April Fools|Original Color|
|-|-|-|
|[0]-[39]|Default Dark Red|Default Gray|
|[40]-[79]|Common Red|Common White|
|[80]-[119]|Uncommon Aqua|Uncommon Green|
|[120]-[159]|Rare Light Purple|Rare Blue|
|[160]-[199]|Epic Gold|Epic Dark Purple|
|[200]-[239]|Legendary Dark Purple|Legendary Gold|
|[240]-[279]|Mythic Blue|Mythic Light Purple|
|[280]-[319]|Divine Green|Divine Aqua|
|[320]-[359]|Special White|Special Red|
|[360]-[500]|Very Special Gray|Very Special Dark Red|
Linked from: [[April Fools]]
